26 template <typename F, Array... A>
27 struct expr_call;

30 struct mapped;

33 namespace detail {
34
35 // Implementation of the nda::get_algebra trait for function call expressions.
36 template <typename... Char>
37 constexpr char _impl_find_common_algebra(char x0, Char... x) {
38 return (((x == x0) && ...) ? x0 : 'N');
39 }
40
41 } // namespace detail
42
43 /**
44 * @ingroup av_utils
45 * @brief Get the resulting algebra of a function call expression involving arrays/views.
46 *
47 * @details If one of the algebras of the arguments is different, the resulting algebra is 'N'.
48 *
49 * @tparam F Callable object of the expression.
50 * @tparam As nda::Array argument types.
51 */
52 template <typename F, Array... As>
53 constexpr char get_algebra<expr_call<F, As...>> = detail::_impl_find_common_algebra(get_algebra<As>...);
54
55 /**
56 * @addtogroup av_math
57 * @{
58 */
59
60 /**
61 * @brief A lazy function call expression on arrays/views.
62 *
63 * @details The lazy expression call fulfils the nda::Array concept and can therefore be assigned to other
64 * nda::basic_array or nda::basic_array_view objects. For example:
65 *
66 * @code{.cpp}
67 * nda::matrix<int> mat{{1, 2}, {3, 4}};
68 * nda::matrix<int> pmat = nda::pow(mat, 2);
69 * @endcode
70 *
71 * Here, `nda::pow(mat, 2)` returns a lazy expression call object which is then used in the constructor of `pmat`.
72 *
73 * The callable object should take the array/view elements as arguments.
74 *
75 * @tparam F Callable type.
76 * @tparam As nda::Array argument types.
77 */
78 template <typename F, Array... As>
79 struct expr_call {
80 /// Callable object of the expression.
81 F f;
82
83 /// Tuple containing the nda::Array arguments.
84 std::tuple<const As...> a;
85
86 private:
87 // Implementation of the function call operator.
88 template <size_t... Is, typename... Args>
89 [[gnu::always_inline]] [[nodiscard]] auto _call(std::index_sequence<Is...>, Args const &...args) const {
90 // if args contains a range, we need to return an expr_call on the resulting slice
91 if constexpr ((is_range_or_ellipsis<Args> or ... or false)) {
92 return mapped<F>{f}(std::get<Is>(a)(args...)...);
93 } else {
94 return f(std::get<Is>(a)(args...)...);
95 }
96 }
97
98 // Implementation of the subscript operator.
99 template <size_t... Is, typename Arg>
100 [[gnu::always_inline]] auto _call_bra(std::index_sequence<Is...>, Arg const &arg) const {
101 return f(std::get<Is>(a)[arg]...);
102 }
103
104 public:
105 /**
106 * @brief Function call operator.
107 *
108 * @details The arguments (usually multi-dimensional indices) are passed to all the nda::Array objects stored in the
109 * tuple and the results are then passed to the callable object.
110 *
111 * If the arguments contain a range, a new lazy function call expression is returned.
112 *
113 * @tparam Args Argument types.
114 * @param args Function call arguments.
115 * @return The result of the function call (depends on the callable and the arguments).
116 */
117 template <typename... Args>
118 21779 auto operator()(Args const &...args) const {
119 21779 return _call(std::make_index_sequence<sizeof...(As)>{}, args...);
120 }
121
122 /**
123 * @brief Subscript operator.
124 *
125 * @details The argument (usually a 1-dimensional index) is passed to all the nda::Array objects stored in the tuple
126 * and the results are then passed to the callable object.
127 *
128 * If the argument is a range, a new lazy function call expression is returned.
129 *
130 * @tparam Arg Argument types.
131 * @param arg Subscript argument.
132 * @return The result of the subscript operation (depends on the callable and the arguments).
133 */
134 template <typename Arg>
135 auto operator[](Arg const &arg) const {
136 return _call_bra(std::make_index_sequence<sizeof...(As)>{}, arg);
137 }
138
139 // FIXME copy needed for the && case only. Overload ?
140 /**
141 * @brief Get the shape of the nda::Array objects.
142 * @return `std::array<long, Rank>` object specifying the shape of each nda::Array object.
143 */
144 669 [[nodiscard]] auto shape() const { return std::get<0>(a).shape(); }
145
146 /**
147 * @brief Get the total size of the nda::Array objects.
148 * @return Number of elements contained in each nda::Array object.
149 */
150 4 [[nodiscard]] long size() const { return std::get<0>(a).size(); }
151 };
152
153 /**
154 * @brief Functor that is returned by the nda::map function.
155 * @tparam F Callable type.
156 */
157 template <class F>

159 /// Callable object.

162 /**
163 * @brief Function call operator that returns a lazy function call expression.
164 *

166 * @tparam As Rest of the nda::Array argument types.
167 * @param a0 First nda::Array argument.
168 * @param as Rest of the nda::Array arguments.
169 * @return A lazy nda::expr_call object.
170 */
171 template <Array A0, Array... As>
172 678 expr_call<F, A0, As...> operator()(A0 &&a0, As &&...as) const {
